## P0 incident — root causes (current focus)
- **P0-B "bot stands still / Manual control"** — TWO independent causes:
  (a) `menu.lua` Widget catch-all made `ui.pause:IsDown()` return a truthy table → `Self Play.lua:1860` paused forever. FIXED by developer 20:15 (real `Widget:IsDown` via GetAsyncKeyState, menu.lua:119-132; loadfile OK).
  (b) loader synthesizer false-positive `OnPrepareUnitOrders` arms `ctx.pauseUntil=now+4` → same mode. loader-events added `[pause] human order armed/expired/RE-ARMED` proof lines + seq dedupe vs our own `orders.prepare()`.
- **P0-A wrong/FROZEN clock** — `gt=9072.1` on 100+ consecutive ticks (frozen; earlier probe read 175.75 same match). Reference `Self Play.lua:1849-1851` returns early while `ctx.now < ctx.nextTick` → brain never runs AND `ctx.pauseUntil` never expires ⇒ P0-A gates both "bot does nothing" and P0-B. Owner api-core (task-15): GetGameTime must be absolute (gs+elapsed, Umbrella contract per architect), advance 1:1 with time, monotonic, `[clock] source=` logged. Exact-clock acceptance: NEW match entered from lobby with loop ALREADY RUNNING (m_flGameStartTime 0→value anchor); current mid-joined match is only sanity (gt > gs and advances).
- **P0-C purchase orders** — 101× `[order] PUO type=16 tgt=0 abil=0` while `[SelfPlay buy] magic_stick` repeats; reference `self_play/shop.lua:844` passes itemId as BOTH targetIndex and abilityIndex. Owner orders-nav (task-16).
- **Integration blockers from QA reload 20:15:40** (owners fixing, QA holds reload):
  - `loader.lua:440 poll error: bad argument #2 to 'VirtualQuery' (cannot convert 'struct 211' to 'struct 177 *')` — duplicate FFI cdef: api.lua:15 declares `VirtualQuery(..., API_MBI*, ...)`, loader.lua:427 redeclares with `EV_MBI*` → first prototype wins. Fix: reuse shared readable(), raw function-pointer cast, or single shared cdef.
  - `api.lua:77 OnUpdate error: attempt to perform arithmetic on 'void *' and 'number'` every tick — `idn + 0x10` on void*; fix = cast to `uint8_t*` first. Blocks the bot brain entirely.

## Permanent rules (human + lead)
1. Live runtime tests ONLY in a single-player bot game (Игра против ботов → ОДИНОЧНАЯ).
2. Test hero is ALWAYS **Treant Protector** (`npc_dota_hero_treant`); other heroes = invalid evidence, remake.
3. Only QA (`ui-hud`) touches runtime (Dota, run.txt/stop.txt, loops); one loop at a time; every window announced to PM.
4. Nothing ships without reviewer PASS + QA evidence (log line / screenshot / QA_REPORT.md).
5. Native changes need compile + reviewer PASS + Lead go-ahead + QA rebuild window.
6. QA regression ⇒ reopen item, revert, re-verify; record why here.
7. PM never edits source/runtime; stuck >20 min ⇒ PM decides revert/alternate/park.
8. One item in flight per owner; gameplay-visible first: clock/pause > purchase/nav > events/perf > HUD > GUI > matchmaking.
9. Agents must loadfile-check every touched Lua file before declaring ready; PM/QA verify before windows.
